MainActivity.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual

import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.navigation.compose.NavHost
import androidx.navigation.compose.composable
import androidx.navigation.compose.rememberNavController
import com.example.etmanual.ui.screens.*
import androidx.navigation.NavType
import androidx.navigation.navArgument
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.TtsViewModel
import androidx.activity.viewModels

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val ttsViewModel: TtsViewModel by viewModels()

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        setContent {
            MaterialTheme {
                AppNavigation()
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AppNavigation() {
    val navController = rememberNavController()

    NavHost(navController = navController, startDestination = "splash") {
        composable("splash") { SplashScreen(navController) }
        composable("login") { LoginScreen(navController) }
        composable("language_selection") {
            LanguageSelectionScreen(navController) { selectedLang ->
                // You could save to datastore here
            }
        }
        composable("home") {
            HomeScreen(navController) }  // <- pass navController here
        composable("actionStoriesList") { ActionStoriesListScreen(navController) } // add navigation target
        composable(
            route = "vocabularyList/{folderNameEncoded}",
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("folderNameEncoded") { type = NavType.StringType })
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val folderNameEncoded = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("folderNameEncoded") ?: ""
            VocabularyListScreen(navController, folderNameEncoded)
        }
        composable(
            route = "quizScreen/{folderNameEncoded}/{startIndex}",
            arguments = listOf(
                navArgument("folderNameEncoded") { type = NavType.StringType },
                navArgument("startIndex") { type = NavType.IntType }
            )
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val folderNameEncoded = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("folderNameEncoded") ?: ""
            val startIndex = backStackEntry.arguments?.getInt("startIndex") ?: 0
            QuizScreen(navController, folderNameEncoded, startIndex)
        }
        composable(
            route = "fullStory/{storyFolderEncoded}",
            arguments = listOf(navArgument("storyFolderEncoded") { type = NavType.StringType })
        ) { backStackEntry ->
            val storyFolderEncoded = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("storyFolderEncoded") ?: ""
            FullStoryScreen(
                navController = navController,
                storyFolderEncoded = storyFolderEncoded
            )
        }
    }
}

Theme.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import android.os.Build
import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicDarkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicLightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext

private val DarkColorScheme = darkColorScheme(
    primary = Purple80,
    secondary = PurpleGrey80,
    tertiary = Pink80
)

private val LightColorScheme = lightColorScheme(
    primary = Purple40,
    secondary = PurpleGrey40,
    tertiary = Pink40

    /* Other default colors to override
    background = Color(0xFFFFFBFE),
    surface = Color(0xFFFFFBFE),
    onPrimary = Color.White,
    onSecondary = Color.White,
    onTertiary = Color.White,
    onBackground = Color(0xFF1C1B1F),
    onSurface = Color(0xFF1C1B1F),
    */
)

@Composable
fun ETManualTheme(
    darkTheme: Boolean = isSystemInDarkTheme(),
    // Dynamic color is available on Android 12+
    dynamicColor: Boolean = true,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val colorScheme = when {
        dynamicColor && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S -> {
            val context = LocalContext.current
            if (darkTheme) dynamicDarkColorScheme(context) else dynamicLightColorScheme(context)
        }

        darkTheme -> DarkColorScheme
        else -> LightColorScheme
    }

    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = colorScheme,
        typography = Typography,
        content = content
    )
}

ActionStoriesListViewModel.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.etmanual.utils.loadTranslations
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import org.json.JSONObject

class ActionStoriesListViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val _translations = MutableStateFlow(JSONObject())
    val translations: StateFlow<JSONObject> = _translations

    fun loadTranslations() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val context: Context = getApplication<Application>().applicationContext
            try {
                val json = loadTranslations(context, "ActionStoriesList.json")
                _translations.value = json
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }
}

//code 2
package com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import com.example.etmanual.utils.*
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

class FullStoryViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val _storySteps = MutableStateFlow<List<String>>(emptyList())
    val storySteps: StateFlow<List<String>> = _storySteps

    private val _storyImages = MutableStateFlow<List<String>>(emptyList())
    val storyImages: StateFlow<List<String>> = _storyImages

    private val _storyTitle = MutableStateFlow<String>("")
    val storyTitle: StateFlow<String> = _storyTitle

    private val _currentStoryFolderName = MutableStateFlow("")
    val currentStoryFolderName: StateFlow<String> = _currentStoryFolderName

    fun loadStory(storyFolderName: String) {

        _currentStoryFolderName.value = storyFolderName

        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val context = getApplication<Application>().assets

                // --- Load JSON once ---
                val jsonText = context.open("translationJSON/FullStoryList.json")
                    .bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
                loadFullStoryJson(jsonText) // top-level function

                // --- Get translated steps and title ---
                _storySteps.value = getFullStorySteps(storyFolderName)
                _storyTitle.value = getFullStoryTitle(storyFolderName)

                // --- Define possible folder variants (for case and punctuation differences) ---
                val basePath = "OriginalActionStories"
                val possibleVariants = listOf(
                    storyFolderName,
                    storyFolderName.uppercase(),
                    storyFolderName.lowercase(),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", ""),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", "").replace(" ", "_"),
                    storyFolderName.replace(" ", "_"),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", "").replace(" ", ""),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", "").replace(" ", "_").uppercase(),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", "").replace(" ", "_").lowercase(),
                    storyFolderName.replace(".", "").trim()
                )

                var foundImages: List<String> = emptyList()
                var matchedVariant: String? = null

                println("DEBUG: Checking all variants for → $storyFolderName")
                println("DEBUG: Top-level folders: ${context.list(basePath)?.joinToString() ?: "None"}")

                // --- Try all folder name variants until one works ---
                for (variant in possibleVariants) {
                    val path = "$basePath/$variant"
                    val files = context.list(path)
                    if (!files.isNullOrEmpty()) {
                        matchedVariant = variant
                        println("✅ DEBUG: Found matching folder variant → $path")
                        println("✅ DEBUG: Found images → ${files.joinToString()}")
                        foundImages = files.sortedWith(compareBy {
                            val numPart = it.substringBefore(".").takeWhile { ch -> ch.isDigit() }
                            numPart.toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE
                        }).map { "$path/$it" }
                        break
                    } else {
                        println("❌ DEBUG: Tried variant → $path → No files found")
                    }
                }

                if (foundImages.isEmpty()) {
                    println("⚠️ DEBUG: No images found in any variant for $storyFolderName")
                } else {
                    println("✅ DEBUG: Using folder variant '$matchedVariant' with ${foundImages.size} images")
                }

                _storyImages.value = foundImages

            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
                _storySteps.value = emptyList()
                _storyImages.value = emptyList()
                _storyTitle.value = storyFolderName
            }
        }
    }
    /**
     * Helper so the UI can ask for a translated line by index (0-based)
     * without needing to pass the folder name manually.
     */
    fun getTranslatedLine(lineIndex: Int, language: String = LanguageStore.selectedLanguage): String {
        val folder = _currentStoryFolderName.value
        // translateFullStoryLine should be the function that reads the JSON by folder/title and returns the requested line
        return translateFullStoryLine(lineIndex = lineIndex, storyFolderName = folder, language = language)
    }
}

package com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.Context
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel

class HomeScreenViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {

    private val _translations = MutableStateFlow(JSONObject())
    val translations: StateFlow<JSONObject> = _translations

    fun loadTranslations() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val context: Context = getApplication<Application>().applicationContext
            try {
                val json = loadTranslations(context, "HomeScreen.json")
                _translations.value = json
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }
}



TtsViewModel.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import java.util.*

class TtsViewModel(application: Application) :
    AndroidViewModel(application), TextToSpeech.OnInitListener {

    private var tts: TextToSpeech? = null
    private val _isReady = MutableStateFlow(false)
    val isReady: StateFlow<Boolean> = _isReady

    init {
        // Initialize TTS ONCE for the whole app
        tts = TextToSpeech(application.applicationContext, this)
    }

    override fun onInit(status: Int) {
        if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
            tts?.language = Locale.ENGLISH // Or dynamic locale if needed
            _isReady.value = true
        }
    }

    fun speak(text: String) {
        if (_isReady.value) {
            tts?.speak(text, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "ttsId")
        }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        tts?.stop()
        tts?.shutdown()
        super.onCleared()
    }
}

//code 4
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.GridCells
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.LazyVerticalGrid
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.items
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.navigation.NavController
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.ActionStoriesListViewModel
import com.example.etmanual.utils.*
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun ActionStoriesListScreen(navController: NavController) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val viewModel: ActionStoriesListViewModel = viewModel()

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { viewModel.loadTranslations() }
    val translations by viewModel.translations.collectAsState()

    val assetManager = context.assets
    val storyFolders: List<String> = try {
        assetManager.list("StoriesPics")
            ?.sortedBy { folder ->
                // Extract the numeric prefix before "."
                folder.substringBefore(".")
                    .trim()
                    .toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE
            } ?: emptyList()
    } catch (e: Exception) { emptyList() }

    val filteredFolders = storyFolders.filter { folderName ->
        translations.optJSONObject("ActionStoriesList")?.has(folderName) == true
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    // Show the translated title only when JSON is loaded
                    val title = if (translations.length() > 0) {
                        translations.translate("ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
                    } else {
                        "ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES" // fallback while loading
                    }
                    Text(title)
                },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = { navController.navigate("home") }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back")
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { paddingValues ->
        LazyVerticalGrid(
            columns = GridCells.Fixed(2),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
            modifier = Modifier.padding(paddingValues)
        ) {
            items(filteredFolders) { folderName ->
                // Load first image in folder
                val images : List<String> = try {
                    assetManager.list("StoriesPics/$folderName")?.sorted()?.toList() ?: emptyList()
                } catch (e: Exception) { emptyList() }

                if (images.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val bitmap by produceState<android.graphics.Bitmap?>(initialValue = null, key1 = folderName) {
                        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                            try {
                                val path = "StoriesPics/$folderName/${images.firstOrNull()}"
                                val bmp = path?.let { p ->
                                    context.assets.open(p).use { stream ->
                                        BitmapFactory.decodeStream(stream)
                                    }
                                }
                                // Only update value on main thread
                                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { value = bmp }
                            } catch (e: Exception) {
                                e.printStackTrace()
                                withContext(Dispatchers.Main) { value = null }
                            }
                        }
                    }


                    Column(
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable {
                                // Navigate to VocabularyList screen
                                navController.navigate("vocabularyList/$folderName")
                            }
                    ) {
//                        bitmap?.let {
//                            Image(
//                                bitmap = it.asImageBitmap(),
//                                contentDescription = folderName,
//                                modifier = Modifier
//                                    .size(120.dp)
//                                    .clip(CircleShape)
//                            )
//                        }
//
//                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
//
//                        val displayTitle = translations.translateStoryTitle(folderName, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
//                        Text(
//                            text = displayTitle,
//                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
//                        )
                        //sub for above
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(120.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            if (bitmap != null) {
                                Image(
                                    bitmap = bitmap!!.asImageBitmap(),
                                    contentDescription = folderName,
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                )
                            } else {
                                // Optional loading placeholder (prevents crash during recomposition)
                                CircularProgressIndicator(
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(40.dp),
                                    strokeWidth = 3.dp
                                )
                            }
                        }
                        //____________________________________________

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                        val displayTitle = translations.translateStoryTitle(folderName, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
                        Text(
                            text = displayTitle,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
                        )

                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

//CODE 11 works great some minor issues with tts delay on and offline
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.net.Uri
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.verticalScroll
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.PlayArrow
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.navigation.NavController
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.FullStoryViewModel
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import java.util.*

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun FullStoryScreen(
    navController: NavController,
    storyFolderEncoded: String,
    viewModel: FullStoryViewModel = viewModel()
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val storyFolder = Uri.decode(storyFolderEncoded)
    val scrollState = rememberScrollState()

    val storySteps by viewModel.storySteps.collectAsState()
    val storyImages by viewModel.storyImages.collectAsState()

    var currentIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }

    // --- TTS instance ---
    val ttsInstance = remember { mutableStateOf<TextToSpeech?>(null) }

    // --- Play All state ---
    var isPlayingAll by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var playJob by remember { mutableStateOf<Job?>(null) }

    // Initialize TTS
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        ttsInstance.value = TextToSpeech(context) { status ->
            if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
                val ttsLocale = when (LanguageStore.selectedLanguage) {
                    "en" -> Locale.ENGLISH
                    "pt" -> Locale("pt", "BR")
                    "es" -> Locale("es", "ES")
                    "fr" -> Locale.FRENCH
                    "de" -> Locale.GERMAN
                    "it" -> Locale.ITALIAN
                    "ja" -> Locale.JAPANESE
                    else -> Locale.ENGLISH
                }
                val result = ttsInstance.value?.setLanguage(ttsLocale)
                if (result == TextToSpeech.LANG_MISSING_DATA || result == TextToSpeech.LANG_NOT_SUPPORTED) {
                    Log.e("TTS", "⚠️ Language not supported or missing data: $ttsLocale")
                } else {
                    Log.i("TTS", "✅ TTS language set to $ttsLocale")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Cleanup TTS
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            ttsInstance.value?.stop()
            ttsInstance.value?.shutdown()
        }
    }

    // --- Sort and load story ---
    val sortedStoryImages = storyImages.sortedWith(compareBy {
        val prefix = it.substringBefore(".")
        prefix.toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE
    })

    LaunchedEffect(storyFolder) { viewModel.loadStory(storyFolder) }

    var bitmap by remember { mutableStateOf<android.graphics.Bitmap?>(null) }

    LaunchedEffect(currentIndex, sortedStoryImages) {
        val imagePath = sortedStoryImages.getOrNull(currentIndex)
        if (imagePath != null) {
            try {
                val loadedBitmap = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    val options = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = 2 }
                    BitmapFactory.decodeStream(context.assets.open(imagePath))
                }
                bitmap = loadedBitmap
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e("FullStoryScreen", "Error loading bitmap: $imagePath", e)
                bitmap = null
            }
        } else bitmap = null
    }

    val atEnd = currentIndex >= storySteps.lastIndex

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text(storyFolder.replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.\\s*"), ""), fontSize = 20.sp) },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = { navController.popBackStack() }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back")
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .verticalScroll(scrollState)
                .background(Color(0xFFEFEFEF)),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            if (storySteps.isNotEmpty() && sortedStoryImages.isNotEmpty()) {
                // --- Image ---
                bitmap?.let {
                    Image(
                        bitmap = it.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = null,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .height(300.dp)
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .padding(16.dp)
                    )
                }

                Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                    // --- Navigation Buttons ---
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { if (currentIndex > 0) currentIndex-- },
                            enabled = currentIndex > 0,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        ) { Text("Previous") }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))

//                        Button(
//                            onClick = {
//                                if (atEnd) navController.navigate("storyQuiz/$storyFolderEncoded")
//                                else if (currentIndex < storySteps.lastIndex) currentIndex++
//                            },
//                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
//                        ) { Text(if (atEnd) "Go to Quiz" else "Next") }
                        Button(
                            onClick = { if (currentIndex < storySteps.lastIndex) currentIndex++ },
                            enabled = !atEnd, // Disable button at end
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        ) { Text("Next") } // Text stays "Next", no navigation
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    // --- Lines with per-line TTS ---
                    for (i in 0..currentIndex.coerceAtMost(storySteps.lastIndex)) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = storySteps[i],
                                fontSize = 18.sp,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )

                            IconButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    ttsInstance.value?.speak(
                                        storySteps[i],
                                        TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH,
                                        null,
                                        "tts-$i"
                                    )
                                }
                            ) {
                                Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, contentDescription = "Play", tint = Color(0xFF1976D2))
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    // --- Play All / Stop button BELOW all lines ---
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            if (isPlayingAll) {
                                isPlayingAll = false
                                ttsInstance.value?.stop()
                                playJob?.cancel()
                            } else {
                                isPlayingAll = true
                                playJob = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Main).launch {
                                    for (i in 0..currentIndex.coerceAtMost(storySteps.lastIndex)) {
                                        if (!isPlayingAll) break
                                        val line = storySteps[i]
                                        // Speak with QUEUE_ADD so lines don't cut each other
                                        ttsInstance.value?.speak(line, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_ADD, null, "tts-$i")
                                        // Approximate delay between lines
                                        val duration = (line.length * 80L).coerceAtMost(5000L)
                                        delay(duration)
                                    }
                                    isPlayingAll = false
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        Text(if (isPlayingAll) "Stop" else "Play All")
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(50.dp))

// --- New Button: Choose Another Story ---
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            navController.navigate("actionStoriesList") {
                                popUpTo("fullStory/$storyFolderEncoded") { inclusive = true } // optional: remove current from backstack
                            }
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                    ) {
                        Text("Choose Another Story")
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(50.dp))

                }

            } else {
                Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@file:OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)

package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.util.Log
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.navigation.NavController
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.HomeScreenViewModel
import com.example.etmanual.utils.translate
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import androidx.compose.runtime.produceState

@Composable
fun HomeScreen(navController: NavController) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val homeVM: HomeScreenViewModel = viewModel()

    // Load translations once
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { homeVM.loadTranslations() }

    val translations by homeVM.translations.collectAsState()

    LaunchedEffect(translations) {
        Log.d("HomeScreen", "Translations loaded: $translations")
        Log.d("HomeScreen", "Selected language: ${LanguageStore.selectedLanguage}")

    }

    val assetManager = context.assets
    val bitmap by produceState<android.graphics.Bitmap?>(initialValue = null) {
        value = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            BitmapFactory.decodeStream(assetManager.open("HomeScreenPics/action_stories.jpg"))
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = {
                    Text(translations.translate("Home", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage))
                },
                navigationIcon = {

                    IconButton(onClick = { navController.navigate("language_selection") {
                        popUpTo("Home") { inclusive = true }
                    } }) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.ArrowBack,
                            contentDescription = translations.translate("Home", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
                        )
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                bitmap?.let {
                    Image(
                        bitmap = it.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = translations.translate("ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth(0.9f)
                            .aspectRatio(1f)
                            .clickable { navController.navigate("actionStoriesList") }
                    )
                }


                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                Text(
                    text = translations.translate("ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

//code 2
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.navigation.NavHostController
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore

@Composable
fun LanguageSelectionScreen(
    navController: NavHostController,
    onLanguageSelected: (String) -> Unit
) {
    val languages = listOf(
        "English", "Español", "Português",
        "Italiano", "Français", "Deutsch", "日本語"
    )

    // Map display names → ISO codes
    val languageMap = mapOf(
        "English" to "en",
        "Español" to "es",
        "Português" to "pt",
        "Italiano" to "it",
        "Français" to "fr",
        "Deutsch" to "de",
        "日本語" to "ja"
    )

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(24.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "Select your language",
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

        languages.forEach { lang ->
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    // Save selection as ISO code
                    val isoCode = languageMap[lang] ?: "en"
                    LanguageStore.selectedLanguage = isoCode
                    onLanguageSelected(isoCode)

                    navController.navigate("home") {
                        popUpTo("language_selection") { inclusive = true }
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp)
            ) {
                Text(lang)
            }
        }
    }
}



LoginScreen.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.navigation.NavHostController

@Composable
fun LoginScreen(navController: NavHostController) {
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Column(
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(24.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "Welcome to ETNINJA",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
            Button(
                onClick = {
                    navController.navigate("language_selection") {
                        popUpTo("login") { inclusive = true }
                    }
                },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                Text("ENTER")
            }
        }
    }
}

////code 6 works great
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.media.MediaPlayer
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.navigation.NavController
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.VocabularyListViewModel
import com.example.etmanual.utils.translateVocabularyListKey
import com.example.etmanual.utils.translateVocabularyWord
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import java.util.*
import android.net.Uri
import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.compose.animation.core.tween
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class, ExperimentalAnimationApi::class)
@Composable
fun QuizScreen(
    navController: NavController,
    folderNameEncoded: String,
    startIndex: Int
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val folderName = Uri.decode(folderNameEncoded)
    val viewModel: VocabularyListViewModel = viewModel()

    // Load translations
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { viewModel.loadTranslations() }
    val translations by viewModel.translations.collectAsState()
    val translationsLoaded = translations.length() > 0

    val assetManager = context.assets
    val images: List<String> = remember(folderName) {
        try {
            assetManager.list("StoriesPics/$folderName")
                ?.sortedWith(compareBy { file ->
                    // Extract the number at the start of the file name
                    val match = Regex("""^(\d+)\.""").find(file)
                    match?.groups?.get(1)?.value?.toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE
                })
                ?: emptyList()
        } catch (e: Exception) { emptyList() }
    }


    var currentIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(startIndex) }
//    var currentIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var currentOptions by remember { mutableStateOf(listOf<String>()) }
    var feedbackColor by remember { mutableStateOf(Color.Transparent) }
    var quizFinished by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // Text-to-speech
    val tts = remember { mutableStateOf<TextToSpeech?>(null) }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        tts.value = TextToSpeech(context) { status ->
            if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
                val locale = when (LanguageStore.selectedLanguage) {
                    "en" -> Locale.ENGLISH
                    "pt" -> Locale("pt", "PT")
                    "es" -> Locale("es", "ES")
                    "fr" -> Locale.FRENCH
                    "de" -> Locale.GERMAN
                    "it" -> Locale.ITALIAN
                    "ja" -> Locale.JAPANESE
                    else -> Locale.ENGLISH
                }
                tts.value?.language = locale
            }
        }
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose { tts.value?.stop(); tts.value?.shutdown() }
    }

    fun prepareOptions(correctWord: String) {
        val allWords = mutableListOf<String>()
        images.forEach { file ->
            val rawWord = file.substringBeforeLast(".").replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "").trimEnd('.').trim()
            val translated = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWord, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
            if (translated != correctWord) allWords.add(translated)
        }
        val wrongWords = allWords.shuffled().take(8)
        currentOptions = (wrongWords + correctWord).shuffled()
    }

    if (translationsLoaded && images.isNotEmpty() && currentOptions.isEmpty() && !quizFinished) {
        val rawWord = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".").replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "").trimEnd('.').trim()
        val displayWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWord, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
        prepareOptions(displayWord)
    }

    fun playSound(fileName: String) {
        val afd = context.assets.openFd(fileName)
        val player = MediaPlayer()
        player.setDataSource(afd.fileDescriptor, afd.startOffset, afd.length)
        player.prepare()
        player.setOnCompletionListener { it.release() }
        player.start()
    }

    if (!translationsLoaded) {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            CircularProgressIndicator()
        }
        return
    }

    val titleTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey(folderName, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
    val backTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey("Back", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text(titleTranslated) },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = { navController.popBackStack() }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = backTranslated)
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues)
                .background(feedbackColor)
        ) {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Top,
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
            ) {
                if (images.isNotEmpty()) {
                    // Top Image
                    val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeStream(
                        assetManager.open("StoriesPics/$folderName/${images[currentIndex]}")
                    )
                    Image(
                        bitmap = bitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = null,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .height(300.dp)
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .padding(16.dp)
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    // 3x3 Grid
                    Column {
                        currentOptions.chunked(3).forEach { rowItems ->
                            Row(
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(4.dp)
                            ) {
                                rowItems.forEach { word ->
                                    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
//                                    Card(
//                                        modifier = Modifier
//                                            .weight(1f)
//                                            .padding(4.dp)
//                                            .height(80.dp)
//                                            .clickable {
//                                                val rawWord = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
//                                                    .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
//                                                    .trimEnd('.').trim()
//                                                val correctWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWord, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
//                                                tts.value?.speak(word, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "WORD_TTS")
//
//                                                if (word == correctWord) {
//                                                    playSound("Sounds/bling.mp3")
//                                                    feedbackColor = Color(0xFFAAFFAA)
//                                                    scope.launch {
//                                                        kotlinx.coroutines.delay(500)
//                                                        feedbackColor = Color.Transparent
//                                                        if (currentIndex < images.lastIndex) {
//                                                            currentIndex++
//                                                            val nextRaw = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
//                                                                .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
//                                                                .trimEnd('.').trim()
//                                                            val nextWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(nextRaw, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
//                                                            prepareOptions(nextWord)
//                                                        } else {
//                                                            quizFinished = true
//                                                        }
//                                                    }
//                                                } else {
//                                                    playSound("Sounds/error.mp3")
//                                                    feedbackColor = Color(0xFFFF4444)
//                                                    val rawWordCurrent = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
//                                                        .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
//                                                        .trimEnd('.').trim()
//                                                    val correctWordCurrent = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWordCurrent, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
//                                                    prepareOptions(correctWordCurrent)
//                                                }
//                                            },
//                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
//                                    )

                                    Card(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .weight(1f)
                                            .padding(4.dp)
                                            .height(80.dp)
                                            .clickable {
                                                val rawWord = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
                                                    .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
                                                    .trimEnd('.').trim()
                                                val correctWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWord, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)

                                                scope.launch {
                                                    if (word == correctWord) {
//                                                        playSound("Sounds/bling.mp3")
                                                        feedbackColor = Color(0xFFAAFFAA)
                                                        delay(300) // small delay before TTS
                                                        tts.value?.speak(word, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "WORD_TTS")
                                                        delay(500)
                                                        feedbackColor = Color.Transparent
                                                        if (currentIndex < images.lastIndex) {
                                                            currentIndex++
                                                            val nextRaw = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
                                                                .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
                                                                .trimEnd('.').trim()
                                                            val nextWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(nextRaw, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
                                                            prepareOptions(nextWord)
                                                        } else {
                                                            quizFinished = true
                                                        }
                                                    } else {
//                                                        playSound("Sounds/error.mp3")
                                                        feedbackColor = Color(0xFFFF4444)
                                                        delay(300) // small delay before TTS
                                                        tts.value?.speak(word, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "WORD_TTS")
                                                        val rawWordCurrent = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
                                                            .replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "")
                                                            .trimEnd('.').trim()
                                                        val correctWordCurrent = translations.translateVocabularyWord(rawWordCurrent, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
                                                        prepareOptions(correctWordCurrent)
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            },
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp)
                                    )
                                    {
                                        Box(
                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .fillMaxSize()
                                                .background(Color.White)
                                        ) {
                                            Text(word)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // 🔽 Animated "Next Screen" Button Appears Here
                    AnimatedVisibility(
                        visible = quizFinished,
                        enter = fadeIn(animationSpec = tween(700)) +
                                slideInVertically(initialOffsetY = { it / 2 }, animationSpec = tween(700)),
                        exit = fadeOut(animationSpec = tween(300))
                    ) {
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))
                        Button(onClick = { navController.navigate("fullStory/${Uri.encode(folderName)}") }) {
                            Text(
                                translations.translateVocabularyListKey(
                                    "Go to Full Story",
                                    LanguageStore.selectedLanguage
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

SplashScreen.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.res.painterResource
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.navigation.NavHostController
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import com.example.etmanual.R

@Composable
fun SplashScreen(navController: NavHostController) {
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        delay(2000) // 2 seconds
        navController.navigate("login") {
            popUpTo("splash") { inclusive = true }
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
            Image(
                painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground),
                contentDescription = "App Logo",
                modifier = Modifier.size(120.dp)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Text("ET App", style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium)
        }
    }
}

//code 5 some issues with tts but works great otherwise
package com.example.etmanual.ui.screens

import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.asImageBitmap
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.navigation.NavController
import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore
import com.example.etmanual.ui.viewmodel.VocabularyListViewModel
import com.example.etmanual.utils.translateVocabularyWord
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.ArrowBack
import android.net.Uri
import com.example.etmanual.utils.translateVocabularyListKey
import android.speech.tts.TextToSpeech
import java.util.Locale
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.animation.AnimatedVisibility
import androidx.compose.animation.fadeIn
import androidx.compose.animation.fadeOut

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun VocabularyListScreen(navController: NavController, folderNameEncoded: String) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val viewModel: VocabularyListViewModel = viewModel()
    val folderName = Uri.decode(folderNameEncoded)

    // Load translations
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) { viewModel.loadTranslations() }
    val translations by viewModel.translations.collectAsState()
    val translationsLoaded = translations.length() > 0

    val assetManager = context.assets
    val images: List<String> = remember(folderName) {
        try {
            assetManager.list("StoriesPics/$folderName")?.sortedWith(compareBy {
                val numPart = it.substringBefore(".").trim().takeWhile { ch -> ch.isDigit() }
                numPart.toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE
            })?.toList() ?: emptyList()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    var currentIndex by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var quizReady by remember { mutableStateOf(false) } // 👈 tracks when quiz button should stay visible

    // Right after `var quizReady by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }`
    val imageCount = images.size
    LaunchedEffect(imageCount) {
        if (imageCount <= 1) {
            quizReady = true
        }
    }


    // TTS setup
    val tts = remember { mutableStateOf<TextToSpeech?>(null) }
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        tts.value = TextToSpeech(context) { status ->
            if (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS) {
                val locale = when (LanguageStore.selectedLanguage) {
                    "en" -> Locale.ENGLISH
                    "pt" -> Locale("pt", "PT")
                    "es" -> Locale("es", "ES")
                    "fr" -> Locale.FRENCH
                    "de" -> Locale.GERMAN
                    "it" -> Locale.ITALIAN
                    "ja" -> Locale.JAPANESE
                    else -> Locale.ENGLISH
                }
                tts.value?.language = locale
            }
        }
    }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            tts.value?.stop()
            tts.value?.shutdown()
        }
    }

    if (!translationsLoaded) {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            CircularProgressIndicator()
        }
        return
    }

    // Translations
    val titleTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey(folderName, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
    val previousTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey("Previous", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
    val nextTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey("Next", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)
    val startQuizTranslated = translations.translateVocabularyListKey("Start Quiz", LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text(titleTranslated) },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = { navController.popBackStack() }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back")
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues)
        ) {
            Column(
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
            ) {
                if (images.isNotEmpty()) {
                    // Display Image
                    val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeStream(
                        assetManager.open("StoriesPics/$folderName/${images[currentIndex]}")
                    )
                    Image(
                        bitmap = bitmap.asImageBitmap(),
                        contentDescription = images[currentIndex],
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .height(300.dp)
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    // Display Word
                    val rawWord = images[currentIndex].substringBeforeLast(".")
                    val cleanWord = rawWord.replace(Regex("^\\d+\\.?\\s*"), "").trimEnd('.').trim()
                    val displayWord = translations.translateVocabularyWord(cleanWord, LanguageStore.selectedLanguage)

                    Text(
                        text = displayWord,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium,
                        modifier = Modifier.clickable {
                            tts.value?.speak(displayWord, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "WORD_ID")
                        }
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

                    // 👇 Navigation Buttons (close to the word)
                    Row(
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { if (currentIndex > 0) currentIndex-- },
                            enabled = currentIndex > 0
                        ) {
                            Text(previousTranslated)
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = {
                                if (currentIndex < images.size - 1) {
                                    currentIndex++
                                    if (currentIndex == images.size - 1) quizReady = true // show quiz button permanently
                                }
                            },
                            enabled = currentIndex < images.size - 1
                        ) {
                            Text(nextTranslated)
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

                    // 👇 Animated visibility for "Start Quiz"
                    AnimatedVisibility(
                        visible = quizReady,
                        enter = fadeIn(),
                        exit = fadeOut()
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { navController.navigate("quizScreen/$folderName/0") },
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(startQuizTranslated)
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    Text("No images found in this folder.")
                }
            }
        }
    }
}






TranslationUtils.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.utils

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONObject

// Load JSON file from assets/translationJSON/
fun loadTranslations(context: Context, fileName: String): JSONObject {
    return context.assets.open("translationJSON/$fileName").bufferedReader().use { reader ->
        JSONObject(reader.readText())
    }
}

// Extension function to translate a key
fun JSONObject.translate(key: String, lang: String = "en"): String {
    return this.optJSONObject(key)?.optString(lang) ?: key
}



TranslationUtilsActionStories.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.utils

import android.content.Context
import org.json.JSONObject

fun JSONObject.translateStoryTitle(folderName: String, lang: String): String {
    return try {
        val screen = this.getJSONObject("ActionStoriesList")
        // Use folderName exactly as key, no substringAfter
        if (screen.has(folderName)) {
            screen.getJSONObject(folderName).optString(lang, folderName)
        } else {
            folderName
        }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        folderName
    }
}

// Specific translation for the TopAppBar title
fun JSONObject.translateActionStoriesTopBar(lang: String): String {
    return try {
        this.getJSONObject("ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES").optString(lang, "ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES")
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        "ORIGINAL ACTION STORIES"
    }
}

import com.example.etmanual.data.LanguageStore

private lateinit var fullJson: JSONObject

fun loadFullStoryJson(jsonString: String) {
    fullJson = JSONObject(jsonString)
}

//version 3
fun getFullStorySteps(storyFolderName: String, language: String = LanguageStore.selectedLanguage): List<String> {
    if (!::fullJson.isInitialized) return emptyList()
    if (!fullJson.has(language)) return emptyList()

    val langJson = fullJson.getJSONObject(language)

    // Extract numeric prefix from folder, e.g., "8. EATING CEREAL" → 8
    val folderNumber = storyFolderName.substringBefore(".").toIntOrNull() ?: return emptyList()

    // Find the JSON key that starts with the same number
    val jsonKey = langJson.keys().asSequence()
        .firstOrNull { key ->
            key.substringBefore(".").toIntOrNull() == folderNumber
        } ?: run {
        println("⚠️ DEBUG: No story found for '$storyFolderName' (normalized='$folderNumber')")
        return emptyList()
    }

    val storyJson = langJson.getJSONObject(jsonKey)

    return storyJson.keys().asSequence()
        .sortedBy { it.removeSuffix(".").toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE }
        .map { key -> storyJson.getString(key) }
        .toList()
}




fun getFullStoryTitle(storyFolderName: String, language: String = LanguageStore.selectedLanguage): String {
    if (!::fullJson.isInitialized) return storyFolderName
    if (!fullJson.has(language)) return storyFolderName

    val langJson = fullJson.getJSONObject(language)

    val sortedTitles = langJson.keys().asSequence()
        .sortedBy { it.substringBefore(".").toIntOrNull() ?: Int.MAX_VALUE }
        .toList()

    val storyTitle = sortedTitles.firstOrNull { it.contains(storyFolderName, ignoreCase = true) }
    return storyTitle ?: storyFolderName
}

//fun translateFullStoryLine(line: String, language: String = LanguageStore.selectedLanguage): String {
//    // For now just return original line
//    return line
//}

fun translateFullStoryLine(
    lineIndex: Int,
    storyFolderName: String,
    language: String = LanguageStore.selectedLanguage
): String {

    if (!::fullJson.isInitialized) return ""
    if (!fullJson.has(language)) return ""

    val langJson = fullJson.getJSONObject(language)

    // Find the matching story by folder name
    val storyTitle = langJson.keys().asSequence()
        .firstOrNull { it.contains(storyFolderName, ignoreCase = true) }
        ?: return ""

    val storyJson = langJson.getJSONObject(storyTitle)

    // Line keys in JSON are "1.", "2.", etc.
    val lineKey = "${lineIndex + 1}."
    return storyJson.optString(lineKey, "")
}




translationUtilsVocabularyList.kt
________________________________________________________________________________________________________________________
package com.example.etmanual.utils

import org.json.JSONObject

fun JSONObject.translateVocabularyWord(wordKey: String, selectedLang: String): String {
    try {
        val vocabList = this.optJSONObject("Vocabulary List") ?: return wordKey

        // Go through all sections like "1. Brushing your Teeth", "2. Making Coffee", etc.
        vocabList.keys().forEach { sectionName ->
            val sectionObj = vocabList.optJSONObject(sectionName)
            val wordsObj = sectionObj?.optJSONObject("words") ?: return@forEach

            // Check if the selected language exists in this section
            val langArray = wordsObj.optJSONArray(selectedLang)
            val enArray = wordsObj.optJSONArray("en")

            if (langArray != null && enArray != null) {
                for (i in 0 until enArray.length()) {
                    val enWord = enArray.optString(i).trim().uppercase()
                    if (enWord == wordKey.trim().uppercase()) {
                        // Found a match! Return the translated word.
                        return langArray.optString(i)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        e.printStackTrace()
    }

    // Fallback: if translation not found, show original word
    return wordKey
}

// Translate UI keys like "Previous", "Next", "Start Quiz", and folder titles
fun JSONObject.translateVocabularyListKey(key: String, selectedLang: String): String {
    return try {
        // Check if the key exists at the top level of JSON
        this.optJSONObject(key)?.optString(selectedLang) ?: key
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        key
    }
}
